Totals:
- Item 3 proper (CE-1 to CE-9, CE-11, CE-13, CE-14): about 78-128 agent-days (revision 1: 61-102).
- Plus CE-9b: 3-6 (revision 1: 8-14).
- Plus CE-12: 8-14.
- Optional: CE-RG 4-8, CE-8r 10-20, CE-M 30-50.

Item 2 and items 4 and 5 are separate.

Reviewable publication units:
1. CE-1, CE-2 and CE-4: definitions and generic lemma, no cryptography.
2. CE-3 and CE-5: the compile lemma, the critical path.
3. CE-6 and CE-7: deterministic claims and A2.
4. CE-8: A1 over all Beacons.
5. CE-9 (and CE-9b): the KEM transfer per target.
6. CE-10 and CE-11: the item 2 join and the un-compiled ideal world, published only after item 2's own reviews.
7. CE-12 (and CE-RG): after items 4 and 5.

Each unit gets its guide section, analysis paragraph and independent semantic review before integration (KEM join decision 5).

Critical path: CE-1, CE-2, CE-3, CE-5, CE-9, then item 2 (CE-10), CE-11, items 4 and 5, then CE-12. CE-4, CE-6, CE-7 and CE-8 run in parallel once CE-2 or CE-3 exists. Recommended order: CE-1, CE-2 and CE-4 first; then a 4-6 day spike of CE-3 that covers a Server peer's records, an established Beacon's records (including the reconstruction of item (c)) and `create`, before committing to the compile approach (risk 1).

## 15. Risks and fallbacks

1. **CE-3 (compile) is the linchpin.**
   - Hard parts: the stale-kernel simulation relation across all fourteen source14 kinds; the reconstruction of Server peers and Beacon owners from public data; the identity-seed and metadata frame lemmas; the report machinery's treatment of `.reveal` and `.kernel` reads; dynamic lazy reads; and elaboration memory under `-M8000`.
   - Mitigations: per-handler equalities with `map_run_simulateQ_eq_of_query_map_eq`; never unfold the whole program; and the widened spike.
   - Fallbacks: section 8 (a) or (b).
2. **Kernel reads and the report history.** `revealedAt` and `FieldOriginFailure` read identity-pool reveal observations only, and `GoodKem` reads the KEM transcript. It is still unproved that a `.kernel` read and a Server `.reveal` leave both untouched in the report-instrumented run. CE-A2 depends on it (`kernel_reads_unrevealed`). If it fails as a definitional fact, a projection lemma is needed.
3. **Publicity of eligibility and codec laws.**
   - Every predicate in `designate` must be a function of the transcript. The Server-side key match and the Beacon-side link imply `KeyMatchFinal` and the challenge-ciphertext routing only with `Phase1ReadBack` and `Phase2ReadBack`.
   - Beacon-side eligibility relies on item 2's T and S7. If the owner declines S7, Beacon-side tests need the alias-tolerant partner check through `X25519ClassLaw` (owner input 6), or they are restricted to Server-side tests only.
4. **One-sided T4.** Left-or-right secrecy waits for CE-9b, now limited to T4. Event-based claims do not.
5. **The A1 embedding (CE-8).**
   - It reorders target generation and simulates other Beacons through ServerAuth `.hmac`, which increases `qH` by one per other-Beacon finish.
   - ServerAuth's one-object restriction stays.
   - ServerAuth has one target bundle, so A1 carries `NoBundleFailure_c` unless `phase1Total` is extended or CE-8r is built.
6. **Single object.** ProVerif A1-delayed's cross-object builds and L1 case (iv) remain symbolic-only until CE-M. CE claims must say "per live Server object".
7. **Budget transport.** The per-operation hash counts must match the `S+R+H` ledger. Local HKDF and AEAD are runtime, not free, and must appear in the reductions' bills. Fresh-message coins need the numeric cap `qFreshBytes`.
8. **Family-B contamination.** No CE statement may cite `PqxdhCorrelatedExtractFamily.*`, `jointKdfView`, `fixedHkdfSha512JointStream`, `productionHiddenRoot`, `RawCtxSourceHkdf.rootResponse` or the other fixed-HKDF root consumers listed in item 2 plan §1. Apply the same exact-token and direct-reference scans (item 2 plan risk 9).
9. **Overclaim.**
   - The symbolic map is not a soundness theorem.
   - The SSX origin auxiliaries and the identity auxiliaries are not endpoints.
   - The later assigned-ID equality, which is false for production, must never appear.
   - CE-KT is a key-transfer statement and does not cover `attacker(root)`; CE-LOR is the only left-or-right secrecy claim, for one test session.
   - CE eligibility is narrower than the symbolic one; every comparison with a symbolic secrecy target goes through the corollaries of section 9.
   - The passive-quantum lines have no CE endpoint.
   - The published-tuple readings (A2pub, R1', A1) hold on `NoBundleFailure` runs only, unless owner input 15 is approved.
10. **Name collisions.** Avoid the family-B `PqxdhCommon*` prefix and short names such as `Spec`, `impl`, `resume`, `Decision` and `Choice`; use `CE.Output`, `CE.Claim` and qualified names.
11. **Compute.** Single-module checks only, no full `lake build` before the final matrix, Nix only through the minimal flake, and never `nix-collect-garbage` during proof work.
12. **Codec laws not approved.** If `Phase1ReadBack` is not approved for CE, CE-COMPILE fails for Beacon records (fallback (a) or (b) of section 8) and the public designation no longer implies `KeyMatchFinal`. If `DecoderAgreement` is not approved, the Server router stays a free parameter and the record codec is only partly tied to source14's.
13. **Effort.** The previous join underran by 40-60%. CE-3, CE-8 and CE-11 carry most of the uncertainty.

## 16. Points needing owner input

1. **Server objects.** Version 1 has one live Server object, with claims stated per live object. Should the multi-object extension CE-M (same seed and same `serverKid`, cross-object builds, L1 case (iv)) be built, and should several key IDs for one seed be excluded? Recommendation: one object now, CE-M later if wanted. Several key IDs per seed should stay excluded (E23, one `SERVER_KEY_ID`).
2. **Reveal profile.** Confirm Π = ∅: CE has no reveal command, the only in-scope compromise is the static one of section 6, and the handoff's reveal, freshness and compromise-timing requirements are therefore met vacuously. Confirm that `doc/composition-assumptions.md:33` (reveals of identity, root, current-chain and skipped-key state) may be amended accordingly in CE-13. Confirm also that no component-profile headline (kernel, chain or field reveals) is wanted, since P7 is not approved.
3. **Internal reads as proof devices.** Confirm that `compile A` and the ServerAuth embedding `R_c` may use source14's Server kernel reveal and Beacon kernel read internally (ServerAuth restriction 5 argument). They are never answered to the CE adversary, and `compile A` reads no identity, KEM, prekey or one-time field.
4. **Observation modes.** Mode O (public optional plaintext) for authentication claims and mode P (success bits) for secrecy claims, as recommended. Should the screened mode R be added later?
5. **Selection statement form.**
   - Session selection: the headline is the exact sum `Σ_g` over `N = qAccept·(count+1)` designations. The `N·Adv(B_guess)` wording needs signed-difference variants of the T7 hops and of items 4 and 5 (+1-2 days in CE-4, and additional scope in CE-10 and CE-12). Which form should be published?
   - A2 uses no selection factor (raw reports). Confirm, and confirm the per-ordinal fallback (`Σ_{j<qAccept}`, within the allowance) if the mid-run detection lemma fails.
   - Confirm A1's `Σ_c` over `count+1` distinct reductions and the exact record-key sums with coefficient one.
6. **Eligibility and item 2 scope.** Confirm the public eligibility rules of section 7, including the new Beacon-side PQ-key conjunct. This requires item 2's optional T (`qRoot = 2`) and S7 (every hidden entry ideal), which are item 2 owner inputs 3 and 7. The alternative is to use the existing `X25519ClassLaw` contract in a public alias check. Confirm that CE secrecy claims are published with CE eligibility plus the symbolic-eligibility corollaries (`+ε_A2` on the Server side, `+ε_A1` on the Beacon side).
7. **Secrecy deliverables.** Weak secrecy only (CE-APP: guessing a fresh honest message), or also left-or-right secrecy (CE-LOR), which requires CE-9b (+3-6 days). CE-LOR has one test session; is a multi-test-session statement wanted (a further hybrid factor)?
8. **Honest message model for CE-APP.** Experiment-sampled uniform messages of adversary-chosen length `ℓmin ≤ ℓ ≤ ℓmax`, with a `2^{-ℓmin}` term and a total budget `qFreshBytes`. What should `ℓmin` and `ℓmax` (or `qFreshBytes`) be? Should honest application messages instead be drawn from an arbitrary distribution with min-entropy `h`?
9. **Publication versus production.** Origin events stated on publication (matching ProVerif's `Published`), with unpublished productions bounded inside the INT sums and no new assumption.
10. **Fidelity deviations D1-D4 (section 3.9).** (Later extended to D1-D6, section 62; the authoritative D1-D6 ledger is `doc/impl/production-boundary-ledger.md`, section 112.)
    - D1: `create` runs runtime initialization only at Server birth. Justification: in the vendored libsodium a repeated `sodium_init` after success returns 1 without effect, except on its lock-error path, which D1 treats as a process-level failure under the trusted-primitive decision. The alternative is a formal `ensureInit` idempotence law, which is a new contract.
    - D2: the public getters listed in section 3.9 are omitted, including the Beacon's pre-bundle `identity_pk`, `pq_pk` and `get_prekey_pk`, which cannot be added without a source14 extension.
    - D3: snapshot APIs and the pre-Phase-1 helpers are omitted.
    - D4: the adversary chooses the build payload.
11. **Supplied Server seed.** Confirm `supplied = none` for every CE security statement; the supplied branch keeps only exact source equations.
12. **Family-B endpoints.** Confirm that HB-111/112/114 and the first-record theorems are not CE endpoints under R1 (item 2 owner input 9), and that only their backend-generic components are reused.
13. **Primitive games and idealizations in CE headlines.** Confirm the scope extension to CE of each of the following, per headline:
    - CE-A2, CE-A2pub, CE-R1': joint Ed25519/X25519 weak-EUF (`PqxdhJointIdentity`), `shortRejects` with `setup.wiring`.
    - CE-A1: Gap-DH, the classical programmable R1 Extract-ROM, the classical programmable BLAKE2b ROM, joint weak-EUF, the record and schedule loss terms of `generated_authentication` (whatever primitive games they contain must be listed in CE-13), and its premises `X25519ClassLaw`, `X25519ScalarSpread`, `SourceFullCorrect`, `HasAttachedVerificationAdapter`, `ReadWritable`, `Phase2ReadBack`, `FrameReadBack`.
    - CE-KT: ML-KEM IND-CCA with its correctness error and `FunctionalDecapsulation`, the R1 Extract-ROM, HMAC-PRF, Extract on `uniform32`, `SourceFullCorrect`, `prefixConsistent` inside `setup`.
    - CE-OR, CE-UKS, CE-APP, CE-LOR: the above plus the BLAKE2b ROM and the modified-nonce AEAD IND$ and INT-CTXT games of the CTX components, and whatever item 4's receive-side lift needs (for example `DecoderAgreement`).
    - CE-R1, CE-C3: none (deterministic).
14. **Codec laws.** These are abstract laws of the free `Codec`, not facts about Cap'n Proto, but they are assumptions for the CE line:
    - `Phase1ReadBack` (`C/PqxdhServerAuthNegativeControl.lean:55-60`), so far used only by the negative controls: needed by CE-3 (Beacon associated data) and by `designate`. Without it, see section 8 fallbacks.
    - `Phase2ReadBack` and `FrameReadBack`, approved for A1: extend to CE-3 and CE-5 (Beacon-side ciphertext link).
    - One codec for registration and records: record decoder `codec.frameRead`, record writer `codec.frameWrite`; the Server router either free with `DecoderAgreement`, or derived from `frameRead` (deviation that also needs `DecoderAgreement` to match production).
15. **Phase-1 writer failure after signing.** Choose one:
    - (a) Extend the existing control law `ControlLaws.phase1Total` (`C/PqxdhServerAuthNegativeControl.lean:86`) to the CE security line. CE-A2pub, CE-R1' and CE-A1 then become unconditional, subject to a CE-7 lemma about attempts that fail before or during signing.
    - (b) Default, no new assumption: publish CE-A2 on the signing history unconditionally, and CE-A2pub, CE-R1' and CE-A1 on `NoBundleFailure` runs (a public condition).
    - (c) Build CE-8r, a retry-capable ServerAuth variant (+10-20 days), for an unconditional A1 without (a).
    Recommendation: (b) now; (a) if the owner accepts the law.
16. **Root secrecy.** A1-root, S3-server and S3-beacon are `attacker(root)` targets that a key transfer does not express. Mark them partial (recommended now), or also build the optional root-guess endpoint CE-RG (+4-8 days, after items 4 and 5 or with an extra test hop).
17. **Collision terms.** Confirm that A2 has no collision term (the address-free report failure tolerates equal keys) and that A1's injectivity term is `ε_inj`, bounded by a zero-query joint-EUF reduction, so that no distributional property of key generation is assumed.
18. **Framing of the symbolic relation.** Confirm that the deliverable is a documented event/capability map (sections 11-12), with bounded tier-B results stated on their windows, the quantum lines and capabilities O15, O16, O23, O25-O27 symbolic-only, and no computational-soundness theorem.
19. **Ordering and parallelism.** Confirm the stage order of section 14, in particular the widened CE-3 spike before item 2's S1b, and whether items 4 and 5 may start against CE-11's interface before CE-10 is complete.
20. **Still-open earlier decisions.**
    - KEM join decision 5 (independent review before integration) and decision 6 (existential owner address, unread owner constant).
    - Item 2 inputs 3, 7, 9 and 10.
    - From the October 5 handoff: OD6, OD8 and confirmation of the Rust basis (central branch with `5cbd76e`).

## 17. What this plan does not claim

- It is not an end-to-end theorem, and it claims no completion of items 3 to 5. CE claims that depend on items 4 and 5 are scheduled, not proved.
- No computational-soundness transfer of any ProVerif result. No symbolic result is upgraded to a computational bound, and no computational selection or guessing term is attributed to the symbolic side.
- No root secrecy as `attacker(root)` unless CE-RG is built; CE-KT is a key transfer.
- No quantum or post-quantum authentication claim; CE is classical.
- No published-tuple provenance, R1' or A1 on runs with a failed Fresh bundle call, unless owner input 15 (a) or (c) is approved.
- No forward secrecy against a later disclosure of KEM key `k`, no post-compromise recovery, and no KCI.
- No reveal capability of any kind; the freshness and compromise-timing requirements are vacuous under Π = ∅, not discharged for any non-empty profile.
- No multi-object Server results before CE-M.
- No new primitive game. The codec laws of owner input 14, the optional `phase1Total` of owner input 15 and the scope extensions of owner input 13 are used only after approval; until then the plan states the conditional or fallback form. Item 2's R1 scope extension, its Extract-on-`uniform32` game and `SourceFullCorrect` are inherited exactly as the owner approved them on 2026-10-06.
- No persistence, snapshots, restoration, codec internals, running-code access or primitive implementation claims.
- No production Rust change, so no mutation testing applies.

- **N1.** The Beacon's associated data and the public designation's key and ciphertext comparisons are tied to the native values only through the codec readback laws. `Phase1ReadBack` is defined (`C/PqxdhServerAuthNegativeControl.lean:55-60`) but used only by the negative-control and witness modules, so its use in CE is an assumption that needs owner approval (owner input 14).
- **N2.** The existing raw-accept reports make A2 factor-free over all accepts (H4 response).

## 19. Owner decisions (2026-10-06)

- **Codec laws approved** as explicit codec contracts of the common experiment, listed in both assumption lists: `Phase1ReadBack`, `Phase2ReadBack`, `FrameReadBack`, and one codec for registration and records with `DecoderAgreement`.
- **Bundle-writer failure:** option (b), conditional claims. CE-A2 is unconditional on the signing history; CE-A2pub, CE-R1' and CE-A1 hold on `NoBundleFailure` runs.
- **Recommendations accepted:**
  - one live Server object and one key ID per seed;
  - observation modes O (authentication) and P (secrecy);
  - the exact sum over targets;
  - weak secrecy first;
  - deviations D1-D4, including the omitted public getters (D5 was accepted and LQ-F, which retires D6, was selected on 2026-10-07; the D1-D6 ledger is `doc/impl/production-boundary-ledger.md`);
  - `supplied = none` in every security statement;
  - root-secrecy targets marked partial;
  - the symbolic results as a documented event and capability map.
- **Compile-lemma trial:** run the CE-3 trial first, then re-estimate.
- **Reveal profile:** the classical core has no reveal command. Add a post-run "later quantum" reveal mode for harvest-now-decrypt-later confidentiality. After the run ends, every honest X25519 secret, every DH output and every honest Ed25519 identity secret is revealed, and only confidentiality of the run's records and keys is claimed. Authentication is not claimed in this mode. This mode is the computational counterpart of the passive-quantum ProVerif lines. Its cost must be designed and confirmed before it is built. `doc/composition-assumptions.md:33` is amended to describe exactly this profile.

## 20. Plan change after the CE-3 trial (2026-10-07)

- **Transported read caps (section 8 budget table).** The trial's `compile_bound` (`PqxdhDeploymentCompileBudget`, commit 28160a82 on `codex/deployment-experiment`) gives looser caps than the table: `.inl 8` reveal at most the budget of Server send plus Server raw-receive commands (not `qBuild`), and `.inr` kernel read at most the budget of Beacon send plus Beacon raw-receive commands (not `min(qCreate, qFinish)`). Cause: `peerKernel` and `ownerKernel` in `PqxdhDeploymentCompile` read whenever there is no local copy, including for uninstalled key IDs and non-established owners, and a `none` answer is stored as `none`, so the read repeats on later record commands. Soundness is unaffected: the review's probe instantiates T4 `actual_le_real` on `compile A` with `compile_bound` as `certified`, and `room` uses only the create cap. Decision: accept the looser caps as a plan change; every KEM, root or other bill that counts reveals or kernel reads (CE-9 and later) uses the record-command budgets. Recovering the table's caps would need the reads gated on the public log (for example `log.builds kid` and `log.finishes index`) and an amortized whole-run count instead of the per-command `FinitePartitionQueryBounds.weighted_bound` argument, with changes to the compile, records, invariant and budget modules; that is not a small change and was not done. The other rows of the table are unchanged (hash: `qHash` plus one per record command; identity, KEM, prekey and one-time reads 0).
- **Re-estimate (section 14).** Only CE-3 is revised on evidence: about 7-12 agent-days in total (4-6 spent, 3-6 remaining). All other stages keep their section 14 ranges, unrevised; item 3 proper becomes about 67-110 in total (63-104 remaining). F1 options (b) and (c) have no derived estimate; section 8 fallback (b), +25-45, is the only comparable planned figure.

## 25. Owner ruling (2026-10-07, in Codex's session; relayed in /tmp/bc-coord message 17)

Failed registration receives stay in the CE/registration lane. Claude owns three things: the finish-time raw receives at sequences 2..51 that run before the sequence-one check (beacon.rs:462-469), their source proof, and their extra query accounting (up to 51 KDF callbacks and their shared-hash work per candidate receive). The record lift (Codex) consumes the post-registration seed through a CE-owned hook or a preceding equality/hybrid that preserves these effects. No noninterference premise may replace that proof.

## 30. CE-8 review fix pass and the Beacon-side ciphertext link (2026-10-07, Claude decision)

- Correction to §9 and §10 (line "the eleven fields carry the ciphertext and the PQ key"): ServerAuth's `Session` has no ciphertext field, and the ServerAuth root input carries the KEM shared secret, not the ciphertext. The fix pass (c4ad86ff on `codex/deployment-server-auth`) shows the ciphertext link is not provable at the CE-A1 bound: with a KEM whose decapsulation ignores the ciphertext, a relayed build reply with a replaced ciphertext is accepted while the bound stays small. The root link cannot be stated on the outcome because the experiment keeps no roots.
- Decision: CE-8 keeps the public matching pinned to the Server identity (`A1NoPinnedMatch`, a strengthening of the headline event) and integrates without the ciphertext link. The ciphertext link moves to the Beacon-side symbolic-eligibility corollary of CE-12, which gains a term `ε_ct` next to `ε_A1`. `ε_ct` bounds "an established Beacon of owner `k` accepts with a ciphertext different from session `i`'s while its root input equals session `i`'s (ServerAuth's ghost `Partner`) and accept `i` verified `pk_k`". The route is a per-pair `(i, k)` IND-CCA reduction (the decapsulation oracle on the Beacon's ciphertext detects the shared-secret collision) plus the ideal-world probability of the same event, bounded statistically through R1 lazy sampling of the uniform key (the adversary and other Beacons query a point containing it with probability about `(qHash + qFinish)/2^256` per pair). IND-CCA is an existing assumption; no new assumption or contract. Detailed design at the start of CE-12. `designate` keeps its ciphertext-equality conjunct, so CE-9 is unaffected.
- Owner input 17 asked for a zero-query collision reduction for `ε_inj`. The fix pass built `B^coll_t` with one signing query; it is replaced by the zero-query variant (the reduction holds the colliding seed and signs itself) before integration.
- Note (2026-10-07, confirming review of CE-8 at `1170bf02`): the `ε_ct` event above is stated on root inputs (ServerAuth's ghost `Partner`), while CE-A1 bounds only the public pinned match (`A1NoPinnedMatch`), which states no root agreement because the experiment's outcome keeps no root. CE-12 must therefore also connect the public pinned match to ServerAuth's `Partner`. Options: (a) a ghost-level version of the CE-A1 chain, in which `root_failure` and `typed_root` carry the root inputs of the root-audited run through the embedding; (b) an `ε_ct` event stated on the public pinned match, whose root-mismatch branch (the matched session's root input differs from the Beacon's) gets its own bound. The earlier CE-8 remark that root agreement is not needed by the section 9 eligibility is withdrawn. The CE-12 estimate for `ε_ct` (roughly 3-6 agent-days, guide "CE-8: re-estimate") excludes this step. `ε_ct` and its IND-CCA route are planned, not proved, and the ciphertext counterexample of the first bullet is an informal argument recorded as a negative control, not a Lean statement.

## 39. CE-11a increment 3 review fix (2026-10-08)

The review of `claude/ce11a` 4f502f99 found one blocking defect: `Good` (and `merge`) never read the World's used markers, while the World refuses a hidden install whose marker is set, so `Splits` could not hold for any environment that issues an admitted hidden install (design-v2 §5.9 had listed "used markers = hidden installs performed" in `Good`). Fix (commit d83bb01b): `Splits created reg step inv` and every generic join theorem (`worldStep_step`, `worldStep_keeps`, `run_join`, `run_keeps`, `run_join_bind`, `join`, `c3_shared_cache`, `join_observed`, `join_analysis`, `join_later`, `join_later_public`) take an environment-specific admissibility predicate `inv`, conjoined with the unchanged `Good`, that is `Stable` (reads the World only through `fixedView`: used markers, seed table, Server sender/identity/peers up to kernels, owners up to kernels, which the fixed steps keep: `effects_view`, `serverStep_view`, `beaconStep_view`) and holds at the initialized fresh World (`initialized_view`); with `inv := fun _ _ => True` the old statements follow (`splits_true`, `stable_true`; a scratch probe re-derives all eleven). A toy environment with one hidden Server install that the World admits (`toy_admitted`) satisfies `Splits` relative to `toyInv` (marker set exactly when the environment's flag is) and C1 (`toy_splits`, `toy_join`). Also `join_reveal` (run-phase reveal projection), docstrings marking `realStep` and the split wrappers as intended capability forms, and a `frame_wrapped` note (CE-11b may prove `Frame envG (qInit+32) (allowanceG core ceFirstTouch)` directly). Join module: 66 theorems (was 52). No new assumption or contract. CE-11b's envG supplies its own `inv` (used markers = hidden installs performed, with the C2b admission lemma). Central moved to fa8d9a54 (item 2 increment 3, #165) during the fix; the merge belongs to the integration step. Section 35 was duplicated; the earlier one (increment 2 built) is now 34a.

## 42. CE-11a increment 4 review fix (2026-10-08)

The review of `claude/ce11a` 7bb6dee9 (semantic and hygiene lenses) raised one minor finding and nine nits (one of them a summary of the passed hygiene checks), nothing blocking. Minor: the guide said CE-11b reuses `realReg_splits_create`, `realReg_splits_bundle` and `realReg_splits_hmac`, whose commands the ideal registration "runs unchanged"; but these lemmas and the whole `Commands` section of `PqxdhDeploymentEnvironmentReal` (`createStep_effects`, `create_law`, ...) are stated for `realReg : RegImpl KemSK Unit 0` (`EnvState KemSK Unit`, World `State 0`, `realStep (n := 0)`), while CE-11b works at n = 2 with its own backend, and `idealReg g` differs in the commands that touch the challenge (design-v2 section 6: the create at creation index k uses the pre-generated challenge KEM key, the accept at raw-accept ordinal i performs the challenge encapsulation with a uniform shared secret, and owner k's routed decapsulation of the exact challenge ciphertext returns that shared secret). CE-11b reuses the generic tools (sections `Bridge` to `FinishShape`, generic in n and the backend) and the proof pattern, and re-proves the per-command laws for its handler (or first generalizes them over n and the backend against a generic handler); this revises design-v2 m12's expectation that the `realReg_splits` lemmas themselves are reused. Judgement: CE-11b stays at 17-32, because the 11b-1 row of design-v2 section 6 lists the instance proof `Splits` (C1°) for the ideal handler among the work of 11b-1, estimated at 9-16 (design-v2 sections 6 and 7), and the generic tools and the proof pattern now exist, so the loss of the m12 reuse is judged to fit within 11b-1's range. The real instances run with zero hidden slots, the most direct reason the trivial `inv` suffices. Nit: the World form of CE-11b's hidden splits needs `effects_then` (forwarded part through `effects_ctx`) with an explicit World computation of the hidden install, or a count-freeness lemma for hidden installs, since `buildSplitEnv`/`finishSplitEnv` end with an administrative request and `countFree_issue` covers only known kernels. Fix: commit 3b53ddd1 (`PqxdhDeploymentEnvironmentReal` 140 theorems, +5 real instances of the join projections: `c1_real_analysis`, `c1_real_reveal`, `c1_real_classical_observed`, `c1_real_classical_analysis`, `c6_real_public`; docstrings on the n = 0 scope and on `onNative_run`/`onShared_run` restating `PqxdhDeploymentCompileStep.onNative_run`/`PqxdhRegistrationSchedule.onShared_run`) and one documentation commit (guide, analysis with forward pointers from increment 3, receipt). No statement changed; no assumption or contract. Section 39 was duplicated; the later one (increment 3 integrated; next work) is now 39a. The committed snapshot is refreshed at integration. A confirming review of fbb77bdd found all ten findings resolved and every hygiene check passed, with three documentation nits (the increment-3 analysis pointer named "the next section", which the CE-9b section on central falsifies after the merge; the estimate judgement and the challenge-touching commands above), addressed by one documentation commit before the integration.

## 75. CE-11b unit U3 remainder and unit U4, first part: review fix pass (2026-10-09, track u4)

An independent review (semantic and hygiene) of `codex/deployment-ce11b` at `9c477e45` found no wrong Lean statement (133 theorems with 133 exact guards within `propext`, `Classical.choice` and `Quot.sound`; every module and the root recompiled with exit 0 and empty logs). Major finding: the public-key-only form of `anomaly_le` planned through `ctGuessPk` (section 73, the guide's open points, design-v2 section 26) cannot be consumed by CE-12a's IND-CCA reduction (section 49, Q2, option (b)). `ctGuessPk` is a supremum over all functions of the public key, including inefficient ones, an information-theoretic quantity that no approved assumption bounds (section 40); from `Pr[Anomaly] ≤ ctGuessPk` CE-12a would only get a supremum of advantages of unconstructed guessers, which collapses into option (a). Correction (implementation-level, no new assumption or contract; Q2's decision stands): the public-key-only form is `anomaly_le_pk`, `𝒟[Anomaly … on World.run (envGD t)] {true} ≤ ctGuessBy setup (anomalyGuesser …)` with premises `Phase1ReadBack` and `Phase2ReadBack`, where `PqxdhDeploymentIdealAnomaly.ctGuessBy setup guess` is the guess probability of an explicit guesser program over the KEM's ambient oracles, run on the public key before the encapsulation, and `anomalyGuesser` runs the game prefix from the public key alone up to the first of the selected raw accept and owner `k`'s first finish made in `InitSent` and returns that finish's Phase 2 ciphertext (stopping point corrected after the confirming review, section 77: `InitSent` is a public phase, also visible in the simulated native state; a finish outside `InitSent` is rejected without reading the secret key and leaves the Beacon unchanged, and the first finish in `InitSent` either establishes or aborts the Beacon, `beacon.rs` lines 403-406 and 487-490 and `summarize`, so it is the only finish of owner `k` that reads the secret key, and on `Anomaly` it is the designating finish, `finishOf`). CE-12a packages the guesser as a `OneKeyAdversary` whose pre-challenge phase runs it (`ambientLift`, `guess_preChallenge`) and makes one decapsulation query of the predicted ciphertext. U4's steps: a draw swap that moves the challenge encapsulation behind the run, a cut of the run at that stopping point, the environment without the chosen secret key and the challenge equal to `envGD` up to the cut, and the Beacon converse's prerequisite (the selected session's build response carries `p.challenge`); `anomaly_le` with `ctGuess` follows by `ctGuessBy_le_ctGuess`. Re-estimate: the anomaly item 3.5-6 (was 2-3.5); remaining U4 4.75-8.5 without L2; U4 about 6-10.25 in total. Fix pass: Lean commit `5aff9fa8` (new module `PqxdhDeploymentIdealAnomaly` with the definitions `ctGuessBy`, `encapsHit` and `ambientLift` and 6 theorems: `encapsHit_max`, `ctGuessBy_le` (at most `ctGuessPk`), `ctGuessBy_le_ctGuess`, `ctGuessPk_eq_iSup`, `ctGuessBy_probComp`, `guess_preChallenge`; three premise-free corollaries in `PqxdhDeploymentIdealProvenance`, `ideal_trace_world_peer`, `ideal_trace_peer` and `ideal_slots_restrictions`; docstrings of `PqxdhDeploymentIdealTrace`, `PqxdhDeploymentIdealOrigins` and `PqxdhDeploymentIdealProvenance`; no existing statement changed; 142 theorems in the track's seven modules; 1828 handwritten Lean files) and one documentation commit (guide section "CE-11b unit U4: review fix pass", the analysis's "Review." paragraph, the receipt, design-v2 section 28). Nits fixed: the World-level annotation versus the record lift's slot map (`run_origins`, U7), "replaces" instead of "covers" for `ledger_keeps` (no `gameWith`-level `ledger_keeps` exists), `Phase1ReadBack` as a premise of the whole statement, the root-check wording, the registry notes. Records without action: central moved to `3915c3f0` (a trial merge conflicts only in the three inventory places: the handwritten Lean count, the root's digest row and the digest row of `check-inventory.sh`, whose hunk also carries central's adjacent `trusted-boundary.md` row). Not reviewed; not integrated. Notes: `/tmp/bc-arc-scratch/common-experiment/ce11b/u4/fix/NOTES.md`.

## 84. Anomaly bound route (2026-10-10, Claude decision)

The public-key-only anomaly bound (§49 Q2 option (b)) is built with a guesser that has oracle access to a decapsulation oracle for the selected KEM key, not by re-running the game at another secret key: owner k's finishes before the selected accept decapsulate adversary-chosen ciphertexts, so a key swap changes their results and the runs are not identical until the stop. The guesser draws everything else itself, runs the split game to the selected accept, answers owner k's pre-accept decapsulations through the oracle and outputs the transcript guess. `anomaly_le_pk` bounds Pr[Anomaly] by the probability that this guess equals a fresh encapsulation to the selected key; CE-12a turns that into a predict-then-compare IND-CCA reduction (the reduction answers the guesser's decapsulations with the IND-CCA phase-1 oracle, then decapsulates the guess before the challenge and compares keys), roughly 2·Adv^IND-CCA + δ_corr + 2^-256, existing assumption only.

## 95. CE-11b main line complete (2026-10-10)

Central `ab9986ef`: CE-11b U1-U8 are integrated. The hand-off `PqxdhDeploymentIdealHandoff.ideal_world_view_slots` gives, per target g, 𝒟[item 2's two-slot world] ≤ 𝒟[decision ∧ IdealSlots on the record-lift view of realChains (envGD g)] + 𝒟[Anomaly on World.run envGD]; the anomaly term is bounded by `anomaly_le_pk` (decapsulation-oracle guesser, §49 Q2(b)), which CE-12a turns into an IND-CCA term. `run_origins` relates the record lift's slot map to CE's annotation. Remaining before the classical headlines: CE-12 (design running); for the later headlines also the LQ-F upstream work and 11b-L (design running).

## 96. CE-12 design adopted (2026-10-10, Claude decision)

Design /tmp/bc-arc-scratch/common-experiment/ce12/design-v2.md (three-lens critique resolved). Decisions: (1) ε_ct is restated on a public event (CE-A1's pinned match with a different ciphertext, `CtGap`) closed in the ideal world by `ctgap_world`, so §30's Partner link is no longer needed; the explicit-term form is built first and a 0.5-day spike decides the closed form (if it fails, the Beacon-side corollaries carry Pr[CtGap] explicitly and this is reported to the owner before publication). (2) The anomaly term uses `ptc` (predict-then-compare) with coefficient one. (3) The symbolic LOR corollary filters on the public NoBundleFailure condition (§19) and adds ε_inj and a new ε_ctcoll (one encapsulate-and-compare IND-CCA adversary). (4) The optional agreement fact AG (b2s publisher pin and mode-O plaintext; no contract needed, the replay dictionary is keyed on associated data) is built in P2, so those fields do not stay symbolic-only. Increments: P1 (headlines, running on `codex/deployment-ce12`), P2 (gaps, symbolic corollaries, bills, D-ct, AG), P3 (CE-12-L after the LQ-F upstream). PB-D interface pin sent to Codex (message 415).

## 97. LQ-F upstream design adopted (2026-10-10)

Design /tmp/bc-arc-scratch/common-experiment/lqf-up/design-v2.md (critique resolved, no owner item). Item 2 is re-proved over the logged LQ-F source with one extra grouped request (the logged accept); the post-run HMAC queries are covered by the existing coefficient-one guessing term at (qAccept + qFinish + qHmac + qHmac₂)/2^256 under R1 alone (the post adversary learns every DH coordinate but not the KEM candidate). Units: D (frozen targets), C (11b-L with U-L4 and the LQ-F designation range), A1/A2 (item 2 over the logged source), B0-B2 (`ideal_compile_later`). Estimate 10.25-15.25 nominal agent-days, about 6-9 at measured throughput; this replaces §49 Q1's 14.5-27 and CE-12 v2's 5-12. Running: D then C and A1.

## 98. CE-12 P1 built (2026-10-10, track ce12/p1)

P1 of the CE-12 design (design-v2 sections 3-6 and 11) is built on branch `codex/deployment-ce12` (worktree /tmp/beaconcrypt-deployment-ce12) over central `ab9986ef`, not reviewed and not integrated: nine modules with 116 guarded theorems, one Lean commit each (`PqxdhDeploymentHeadlineChain` 5cc9de94, `PqxdhDeploymentHeadlineAnomaly` ad17f044, `PqxdhDeploymentHeadlineKeyTransfer` dc845b6e, `PqxdhDeploymentHeadlineEvents` ec257abf, `PqxdhDeploymentHeadlineContain` 2e671688, `PqxdhDeploymentHeadlineApp` 3c7e630d, `PqxdhDeploymentHeadlineOrigin` efb68a15, `PqxdhDeploymentHeadlineUks` 32dc5de4, `PqxdhDeploymentHeadlineLor` c0dbcc8d), a documentation-string correction of the events module (68498a95) and one documentation commit (guide sections "CE-12: ...", the analysis section "Common deployment experiment, CE-12 P1: the classical headline theorems", the receipt `doc/impl/proof-receipts/common-deployment-experiment-ce12-p1.json`). Proved: CE-KT (`ce_kt`, `ce_kt_close`, `ce_kt_sum_le`, `ce_kt_sum`, World forms `ce_kt_world*`; bill kemLoss + slotLoss), CE-OR (`ce_or_b2s` without β_g, `ce_or_s2b` with it; record_auth_lift then OriginStatement; + publicationTerm), CE-UKS (`ce_uks_b2s` without β_g, `ce_uks_sender` with it; record_auth_lift then BindingTarget; + bindingTerm BI_g), CE-APP (`ce_app`, `ce_app_server`; record_lift then FreshGuessStatement; + 2^-min), CE-LOR (`ce_lor`, `ce_lor_server`; eligibility anchored on firstTest, two transfers per target, LorStatement through the public hiding projection; ½β_g), each per target and as the exact sum over Fin qAccept × Fin qCreate with coefficient one; `ptc` and `ptc_bound` (ctGuessByCca ≤ Adv^IND-CCA(ptc) + δ_corr + 2^-256, coefficient one; phase 1 at most qFinish + 1 decapsulations, phase 2 none); the per-target chain in U7's names. No new assumption or contract; no import of PqxdhBoundary*. Deviations (recorded in the guide's "CE-12: decisions and deviations"): CE-KT World forms kept (the task named them), UKS realized as receiver and sender event families, LOR stated as ofReal |advantage| ≤ Σ in extended reals. Checks: every module and the root via coord.sh lean into ce12/p1/olean and again at 68498a95 into ce12/p1/olean2 (exit 0, empty logs), guards 116/116, inventory recount before rehash (1884 → 1893), focused check-inventory after every module and at the clean head 68498a95 (exit 0), git diff --check. Next: P1 review and fix pass, then integration (take the newer wholesale plan snapshot at the integration); P2 and P3 as in design-v2 section 11. ## 101. CE-12 P1 integrated: the classical headlines (2026-10-10)

Central `b88e3012`: `PqxdhDeploymentHeadline{Chain,Anomaly,KeyTransfer,Events,Contain,App,Origin,Uks,Lor}` (116 theorems). CE-KT (`ce_kt_sum`, two-sided and World forms), CE-OR (`ce_or_b2s` without the anomaly term, `ce_or_s2b` with β_g), CE-UKS (`ce_uks_b2s`, `ce_uks_sender`), CE-APP (`ce_app`, `ce_app_server`, mode P), CE-LOR (`ce_lor` with a public filter, `ce_lor_server`), each an exact sum over targets with coefficient one, complete bills (kemLoss, slotLoss, β_g = Adv^IND-CCA(ptc) + correctnessError + 2^-256, record-lift losses, final ideal-world terms) and only existing contracts. The review fix dropped an unused DecoderAgreement premise from CE-OR. P2 (gaps, symbolic corollaries, collision terms, bills, D-ct, AG) is running.

## 102. CE-12 P2, first session (2026-10-10, track ce12/p2)

P2 of the CE-12 design (design-v2 sections 7-9) is partly built on branch `codex/deployment-ce12` (worktree /tmp/beaconcrypt-deployment-ce12) over central `b88e3012`, with central `c031e4e7` merged in at `a981309b`. It is not reviewed and not integrated. The D-ct spike ran first: the statement compiles (`ce12/p2/spike/CtGapWorld.lean`), but the remaining work is estimated at 3-6 actual days (first estimated at 4-8; corrected in section 103), above the 1.5-day stop rule. The work comprises a World invariant for the marked build's commitment and material; a Lean lemma for the averaged union bound over the World's dynamic R1 table (the per-entry `2^-256` bound itself holds by averaging over the uniform marginal of `key32_0`, because the Beacon's sequence-one key is a fixed function of the root, `concrete.rs:64-106`, so no random-oracle derivation or independence of `key32_0` from the view is needed; what is missing is proof engineering); the connection of `PqxdhRecordFactsCollision`; and the `c = k*` known-root branch. Items other than the lemma already exceed the stop rule (2-4 days). The stop rule therefore fired: the Beacon-side terms carry the explicit public term `ctGapTerm = 𝒟[CtGapEvent]` (F-ct2). The owner is to be told before publication (design-v2 section 14 note 4). Six modules, 104 guarded theorems:

- `PqxdhDeploymentHeadlineGap` (`361ad9f8`, 30): `SymServer`, `SymBeacon`, `CtGap`, a new run invariant `FinishReady` (`ready_game`, `finish_accepted`), positional sessions, `server_gap`, `beacon_gap`, `server_gap_bound` (`ε_A2pub`), `ce_a1_linked` (`ε_A1 + ctGapTerm`) and `sym_gap`.
- `PqxdhDeploymentHeadlineCollision` (`5553cf77`, 14): `identity_collision_game` (`ε_inj`), `pairAdversary` and `pair_cp_le` (`CP ≤ Adv^IND-CCA(pairAdversary) + 2δ_corr + 2^-256`), and `CtColl` with the explicit term `ctCollTerm`.
- `PqxdhDeploymentHeadlineSymbolic` (`227db4dd`, 25): symbolic events with identity-based counterpart sets, their containments, and `ce_or_b2s_sym`, `ce_or_s2b_sym`, `ce_uks_sym`, `ce_app_sym`, `ce_app_server_sym` and `ce_kt_sym`.
- `PqxdhDeploymentHeadlineSymbolicLor` (`e37ed9e7`, 24): `symbolic_lor` with the NoBundleFailure filter, `|adv_sym − adv_CE| ≤ ½(ε_A2pub + ε_A1 + ctGapTerm + ε_inj + ctCollTerm)`, and `symbolic_lor_bound`.
- `PqxdhDeploymentHeadlineInjective` (`8253a4a3`, 5): `ce_or_injective_b2s` (`+ ε_A2pub`) and `ce_or_injective_s2b` (`+ ε_A1inj + ctGapTerm + ctCollTerm`), with the existing injectivity events CE-R1', CE-C3 and `SharedMatch`.
- `PqxdhDeploymentHeadlineBills` (`4b8a01ed`, 6): `pair_bills` and the record lifts' bills at `BI_g` (`auth_bills`, `records_bills`).

Hygiene commit `3b8a4cd3`: ten no-op deprecation suppressions removed. No new assumption or contract; no `PqxdhBoundary*` import. Checks: every module and the root through `coord.sh lean` into fresh prefixes (`ce12/p2/olean`, `olean2`, `olean3`) and again at the clean heads into `ce12/p2/recheck/olean` and `ce12/p2/recheck2/olean` (exit 0, empty logs, oleans identical to the finals); guards 104/104; recount before rehash (1893 to 1901); focused check-inventory after every module and at the clean head; `git diff --check`. Remaining P2 work, with actual-day estimates:

1. `ct_collision_game`, which closes `ctCollTerm` into `ε_ctcoll` with `pair_cp_le` (2-4).
2. The rest of the bills: `splitStep_uniform`, `ptc_bills` with `guessBill`, and `HeadlineBills` (0.5-1.5).
3. AG (2-5).
4. D-ct, deferred by the stop rule (3-6, section 103).
5. The P2 review, fix pass and integration.

The headlines with `β_g` are gated on `guessBill`, those with `ctCollTerm` on `ct_collision_game`. (Added by CE-12 D-ct, sections 120 and 121: the `ctGapTerm` half is closed by `ctgap_term_le` (`ctGapTerm ≤ epsCt`) and the `_dct` forms of every headline that carried it, with the bills of `epsCt`'s reductions in `ctgap_term_billed` and the `*_billed_dct_full` siblings; not yet integrated, and D-ct's own owner flag is the birthday term `C(ctxCap, 2)/2^512`.) ## 105. CE-12 P2b, step 1: the bills (2026-10-10, track ce12/p2b)

Step 1 of P2b, the rest of the bills of design-v2 section 9 (section 103's item (2)), is built on branch `codex/deployment-ce12` (worktree /tmp/beaconcrypt-deployment-ce12) over central `f0dc46bb` (which carries P2; central did not move), not reviewed and not integrated. Four Lean modules, 71 guarded theorems, one Lean commit each, and one documentation commit: `PqxdhDeploymentHeadlineSplitCost` (`90764af5`, 35): `splitStep_uniform`, the per-step uniform bound of the anomaly guesser's split handler `handlerWith (idealSplit …)` from every extended state, with weights `splitWeight caps` (coin 1, create 64 + keygenCost, bundle 32, accept 140 + encapsCost, build 108 + 64 + 64, finish 69·max(64, decapsCost) + 64, HMAC, BLAKE2b and record commands 64), proved on the split form itself (query bounds do not transfer along `ideal_splits`) from `acceptExposed_cost`, `preparationCE_step`, `markedRoot_uniform`, `createPool_uniform`, `slotRun_total` and new bounds for the native record steps, the hidden build and finish of `erasedHidden` and the seed installs; `PqxdhDeploymentHeadlineGuessBill` (`3b12a590`, 14): `guessWeight` (= design-v2's `u kind`; `test` 129, `fresh` bounds.max + 128), `guessBill` = (qCreate + 1)·keygenCost + (qInit + 32) + Σ_kind B.cap kind · u kind with closed form `guessBill_eq`, and `ptc_bills`: `MakesAtMostQueries guessBill 0 (qFinish + 1) 0 0 0` under `KemBound`, `initBound` and `budget.Respects` (kem_transfer_bills' premises); `PqxdhDeploymentHeadlineBillsFull` (`f147bf69`, 6): `HeadlineBills` (structure of `TargetBills` (kem, slot incl. prf, beta, auth, records), `A1Bills`, `A2Bills`, `InjBills`, `PairBills`) and `headline_bills`, with `a1_bills` deriving CE-A1's bills from `KemBound` and the budget alone; `PqxdhDeploymentHeadlineGated` (`0072644d`, 16): the fifteen frozen headline forms with `β_g` (P1 ce_app, ce_or_s2b, ce_uks_sender, ce_lor per target and summed; P2 ce_or_s2b_sym, ce_uks_sym, ce_app_sym, ce_app_beacon_sym, symbolic_lor_bound, ce_or_injective_s2b, ce_or_injective_s2b_full) restated with the same premises and the bills of their terms conjoined, and `anomaly_beta_billed`. This closes the gate of the headlines with `β_g` on `guessBill` (section 103's owner flag, gating condition); the owner flag on `ctGapTerm` and `ctCollTerm` stands and the headlines with `ctCollTerm` stay gated on `ct_collision_game`. No new assumption or contract; no `PqxdhBoundary*` import; no frozen module changed. Deviations: the weights are the split handler's own rather than read off `idealStep_bound`/`uniformCap`/`OpenUniform.total`; a test is charged at both send classes; the commit messages of `3b12a590` and `f147bf69` miscount their theorems (17 and 7 for 14 and 6). Checks: fresh prefix `ce12/p2b/olean` (mirror of the published `f0dc46bb` first prefix `ce12/p2/int/olean`), each module and the root through `coord.sh lean` (exit 0, empty logs), root again at the clean `0072644d`; guards 71/71; recount before rehash (1908 to 1912); focused check-inventory under `@nix` before every Lean commit (exit 0); `git diff --check`. ## 106. CE-12 P2b, step 2: `ct_collision_game` (2026-10-10, track ce12/p2b)

Step 2 of P2b, design-v2 section 7.4's game-level step `ct_collision_game` (section 103's item (1)), is built on branch `codex/deployment-ce12` (worktree /tmp/beaconcrypt-deployment-ce12) after step 1 (section 105), over central `f0dc46bb` (central did not move), not reviewed and not integrated. Four Lean modules, 95 guarded theorems, one Lean commit each, and one documentation commit: `PqxdhDeploymentHeadlineCtGhost` (`632ca118`, 33): the Server's Phase 1 collector, native prefix and native raw accept split at the encapsulation callback with the encapsulation logged (`collectEnc`, `prefixEnc`, `acceptEnc`, forgetting the log gives the originals), the ghost-instrumented CE handler and experiment (`handlerG`, `gameG`; `game_ghost`: the experiment is `forget <$> gameG`), the fresh-draw expectation lemmas (`acceptEnc_expect`, `create_expect`, `handlerG_expect`, the generic supermartingale lemma `expected_run_le` on VCVio's `expectedValue`), and the potential `phi` of a triple of an owner `k` and raw accept attempts `i < j` with `triple_le` (the ghost collision of the triple has probability at most `CP = pairCollision setup`; `pairHit_mean`: `E_keygen[CP(pk)] = pairCollision`); `PqxdhDeploymentHeadlineCtTrace` (`3aa4a73f`, 44): the run invariant `Tracks` between the transcript, the native state and the ghost (accept, pending token, build response read back under `Phase2ReadBack`, owner key, bundle read back under `Phase1ReadBack`), preserved by every query (`tracks_step`), and `ctColl_triple` (a transcript `CtCollEvent` is the ghost collision of a triple with `k < qCreate`, `i < j < qAccept`, from the transcript counts `game_counts`); `PqxdhDeploymentHeadlineCtColl` (`1483b62f`, 7): `ct_collision_game` (`ctCollTerm ≤ qCreate · C(qAccept, 2) · CP`; premises `Phase1ReadBack`, `Phase2ReadBack`, the budget), `ctCollEps` and `ct_coll_le` (`ctCollTerm ≤ ε_ctcoll = qCreate · C(qAccept, 2) · (Adv^IND-CCA(pairAdversary) + 2δ_corr + 2^-256)`, adding `FunctionalDecapsulation` through `pair_cp_le`), `ct_coll_billed` (with `pair_bills`) and `ctCollEps_le_design`; `PqxdhDeploymentHeadlineCtClosed` (`6c47cc49`, 11): the closed forms of the six frozen headline forms with `ctCollTerm` (`ce_or_s2b_sym`, `ce_uks_sym`, `symbolic_lor`, `symbolic_lor_bound`, `ce_or_injective_s2b`, `ce_or_injective_s2b_full`, each `*_closed`, with `ctCollTerm` replaced by `ε_ctcoll` under the frozen premises, `symbolic_lor_closed` adding `FunctionalDecapsulation`) and of the five billed forms of step 1 among them (`*_closed_billed`, with `PairBills`). Changes from the design, with their justification: the factor is `qCreate · C(qAccept, 2)` rather than `(qCreate + 1) · C(qAccept, 2)`, because only created owners hold KEM keys (the Server holds none) and the transcript counts bound them by `qCreate` (`ctCollEps_le_design` records that the proved bound is at most the design's); the route is a ghost-instrumented copy of the experiment with an expected-potential argument per triple rather than a game decomposition at two accept ordinals, the design's hit set being replaced by the union over triples and the case of an owner created after the earlier accept being covered by the key-generation mass in the potential, so that `CP` is averaged over key generation (exactly `pairCollision`, the quantity `pair_cp_le` bounds). Effect on the owner flag of section 103: the `ctCollTerm` half is closed by `ct_coll_le` and the closed forms (the gate of the headlines with `ctCollTerm` on `ct_collision_game` is closed); the `ctGapTerm` half stands (F-ct2, D-ct deferred). No new assumption or contract; no `PqxdhBoundary*` import; no frozen module changed. Checks: the prefix `ce12/p2b/olean` of step 1, each module and the root through `coord.sh lean` (exit 0, empty logs), the root again at the clean `6c47cc49`; guards 95/95 (scan over the eight P2b modules, 166 theorems); recount before rehash (1912 to 1916); focused check-inventory under `@nix` before every Lean commit (exit 0; the first run for `PqxdhDeploymentHeadlineCtGhost` failed on an unescaped `?` in the generated `rg` patterns of `getElem?_snoc_self` and `getElem?_snoc_ne`, which were escaped as existing rows do, and the rerun passed); `git diff --check`. Central moved during this step (`f0dc46bb` to `c72db84a`, LQ-F upstream unit A2, and `c27b4533`, item 7 increment A); the topic is not merged with it, and a trial merge conflicts only at the usual append anchors (root imports, `reviewed-inventory.txt`, `check-inventory.sh`, guide, analysis). Remaining P2 work: AG (2-5), D-ct (3-6, deferred), then the merge of central, the review, fix pass and integration of P2b (steps 1 and 2); then P3. Guide sections "CE-12 P2b step 2: ...", analysis entry "Common deployment experiment, CE-12 P2b: the ciphertext-collision bound", receipt `doc/impl/proof-receipts/common-deployment-experiment-ce12-p2b.json` (now covering both steps), notes `/tmp/bc-arc-scratch/common-experiment/ce12/p2b/NOTES.md`.

Three Lean modules, 73 guarded theorems, one Lean commit each, and one documentation commit: `PqxdhDeploymentHeadlineAgreeSeal` (`9d202c76`, 41): `handleAd` (a handle's installed record-owner AD as the source builds it: `associatedBytes(Server identity, peer identity)` for a Server peer, the shell AD for an established owner), its stability `AdKept` under every request, schedule, interpreter, native and secrecy-ledger command, and the seal-context invariant `SealAd` on every supported outcome of every ideal CTX world (`ctxRun_sealAd`); `PqxdhDeploymentHeadlineAgreeReceive` (`5d1b98d5`, 20): the receive side, the frozen origin route with the matched seal's record AD bytes kept (`AcceptedAd`: the matched seal's AD is the receiving handle's `handleAd`), the ledger invariant and `ctxRun_entryAd`, with no `FrameReadBack` premise (transcript append by `ledger_append`); `PqxdhDeploymentHeadlineAgree` (`14287522`, 12): `Production`, `AgreeFailure`, `AgreementStatement` and `agreement_statement` (in `idealAuth` the probability that an accepted raw receive on a hidden handle has no counterpart production of its frame agreeing in AD and plaintext is zero, for every environment, mode and adversary, with no premise), `ctxRun_agreement` (positive pointwise form, every ideal CTX world), `ctxRun_agree_zero`, `idealRecords_agree_zero`, `ownsLabel_unique`, `associatedBytes_inj` (injectivity of the extracted `build_associated_data`, through `associatedBytes_exact` and `Pqxdh.assocData_inj`), and the identity forms `ctxRun_peer_agreement`, `ctxRun_owner_agreement` and `owner_pin`. (R2) Mode-O plaintext, b2s and s2b (1.5-3 actual days): a ledger-level seal-source invariant (every dictionary seal at a label owned by a handle comes from a transcript command of that handle with its plaintext) and one seal per frame and handle (label position = sequence − 1, at most one successful seal per label), then a containment in `AgreeFailure` or the unpublished event (`publicationTerm`), b2s through `chain_server`, s2b through `chain_beta`. ## 116. CE-12 P4, step 2: the b2s Server pin on the common deployment experiment (AG lift R1) (2026-10-10, track ce12/p4)

all ten command branches of `idealStep`. `PqxdhDeploymentHeadlinePinControl` (`16956af9`, 19): the record control keeps its Server identity and the admitted associated data of its established shells (`handler_ok`, `effects_program_ok`), and the flattened world's quiet and environment native commands (`nativeImpl_quiet`, `nativeImpl_env`). `PqxdhDeploymentHeadlinePinInvariant` (`0b2720aa`, 20): the two env_g invariants of the P2b remainder as `PinFacts`, (a) every established owner's shell AD is `associatedArray(created pin, some Beacon identity)` and (b) when the control holds a Server every build entry is `BuildShaped` with its identity, on every outcome of every CTX world with `envGD` (`ctxRun_pinFacts`, `idealAuth_pinFacts`; joint invariant `Inv`, `full_inv`); no premise. `PqxdhDeploymentHeadlinePin` (`4ca18db3`, 13): the CE event `PinFailure` (`PinAt g`: an accepted Server raw receive on a peer designating `g` whose counterpart owner `g.2` was created with a pin different from the Server identity read with the Phase 2 reader from session `g.1`'s first build response), `pin_agree` (under `Phase2ReadBack` and `PinFacts`, `dec_g ∧ ISs_g` of it implies `AgreeFailure`, via `counterpart_peer`, the Beacon restriction and `owner_pin`), `idealAuth_pin_zero`, `ce_pin_b2s(_target)` (at most the sum over the targets of `kemLoss + slotLoss + authLoss_g(dec_g ∧ ISs_g)`, coefficient one, no anomaly or publication term) and the CE-OR b2s form with the pin field, `ce_or_pin_b2s(_target)` (the frozen `ce_or_b2s` shape for `OriginFailure .b2s ∨ PinFailure`, with `publicationTerm`). Premises those of the frozen `ce_or_b2s` (`FrameReadBack` only for the combined form); no existing module changed; no new assumption or contract; no `PqxdhBoundary*` import. Deviation: the containment is proved directly into `AgreeFailure` (its identity form) rather than through `ctxRun_peer_agreement`, the positive form of the same fact. ## 122. CE-12 D-ct: confirming review (2026-10-10, track ce12/dct)

A confirming review of the D-ct review fix pass (section 121: Lean commit `10c557cb`, documentation commit `78055c37`) at `78055c37` (read only, scratch `ce12/dct/confirm`) found every finding of the review at `e291c58f` resolved and no statement weaker. The Lean changes since `e291c58f` are the module docstring of `PqxdhDeploymentHeadlineDctClosedLater` and, in `PqxdhDeploymentHeadlineDctClosedLaterP4`, its module docstring and the four `*_billed_dct_full` siblings with their guards; against `427758b6` no published module changed (added: the fourteen D-ct modules and the receipt; changed: the root import block, the two inventory files, the analysis and the guide). A receipt check (`ce12/dct/confirm/tools/receiptcheck.py`): the fourteen module sha256 sums, theorem lists and counts, the three file hashes and the olean artifacts equal the receipt; 287 theorems with 287 exact guards in order, axioms within `propext`, `Classical.choice`, `Quot.sound` (four depend on none); no `sorry`, `admit` or axiom; the 17 root-check records match (`head_before_commit`, dirty count, root sha256); the reviewed-inventory rows of the D-ct files, `check-inventory.sh` and the root match (16 rows; handwritten Lean 1972). A Lean comparison `CmpFull.lean` (through `coord.sh lean` against `ce12/dct/impl/leanpath3`, exit 0) of the four siblings: binders equal, first conjunct the conclusion of the `_dct` form, second conjunct `ctgap_term_billed`'s at mode `.P` and adversary `first`, as in `epsCt`; 4/4. `PqxdhDeploymentHeadlineDctClosedLaterP4` and `PqxdhDeploymentHeadlineDctClosedLater` recompiled without `-o` (exit 0, empty logs); the twelve billed `_dct` forms of P2b each state `TargetBills` at their `epsCt`'s mode and adversary. Verdict: approve with nits. Two nits, taken by the commit that records the review on branch `codex/deployment-ce12-dct` (documents and receipt only, no Lean change): (1) the documents at `78055c37` said that the integration waits for the owner's acknowledgment of the birthday term, which conflicts with the later decision of Claude's orchestrator (2026-10-10): the birthday term `C(ctxCap, 2)/2^512` in place of design-v2 section 8's `H/2^512` is a proof-internal bound choice (a weaker bound, not a new assumption, contract or scope change), covered by the owner's standing delegation of 2026-10-07. The decision stands, so every place carries the recorded note: reported to the owner on 2026-10-10; accepted by Claude under the owner's 2026-10-07 delegation of proof-internal choices; the linear form would need a ChaCha20-Poly1305 tag or X25519 coordinate unpredictability property, which is not assumed, and extra guess terms; the birthday term is below `2^-385` for `ctxCap ≤ 2^64` (the third clause follows design section 2 and amendment A2, which say that a single AEAD property would not suffice). Reworded in the guide (open point 1, the status paragraph, "CE-12 D-ct: decisions and deviations", a superseding note on the fix pass's finding 1), the analysis (six pointers, the D-ct entry and its new paragraph "Owner note") and the receipt (`owner_flags[0]`, `open_points`, superseding notes on review findings 1 and 9); the term does not gate the integration, and the integration report carries the note with design owner notes 2 to 4. Sections 120 and 121 ("Owner flag (before publication, ...)", "gating the integration", "after the owner's acknowledgment") are kept as written and are read with this note. (2) Four more statements of the kind finding 4 targeted had no D-ct pointer: the guide's status paragraphs of P2b step 1 and P4 step 3, and the not-claimed paragraphs of the analysis's P4 step 2 and step 3 entries; pointers added, and at the optional fifth place, the guide's bullet "F-ct2 instead of the closed `ε_ct`" of "CE-12 P2: decisions and deviations". The record commit also adds the guide section "CE-12 D-ct: confirming review", records the review in the status paragraph, open point 2, the analysis and the receipt (`review.confirming_review`, `record`, `plan_snapshot`), and refreshes the committed plan snapshot `doc/impl/common-deployment-experiment-plan.md` as a wholesale copy of this plan through this section (central's snapshot was CE-12 P4's, through section 119). Next: the integration into central (PROTOCOL section 6); central is still `427758b6`, merged into the topic at `877939df`, unless it moves first.
